source dispatch moves one Arena and Usage through actual framing, versioned archive integrity, exact owned canonical basis identity, direct program admission, Product.MachineBinding execution, and ordered ObjectDemux output. Raw bodies use the same ordered output without fabricating a program. No recursive reference parser or Graph/Trie basis fallback is used. Pending bytes require explicit acknowledgement; takeSpan outcome transfers a separate image owner to the consumer aggregate. Publication failure freezes pending event and custody. Cleanup routes each actual cursor, detached release, Core cancellation and trial-owned terminal roots before clean. Before parsing or cleanup, a prepaid fixed-state audit scans actual arena index entries and matches root multiplicity to the archive ticket, standalone owned basis record if any, and the fixed seven protected caller fields. Identical handles in distinct ticket roles count separately. Ownership refusal retains the entire achieved session and cannot become archive exclusion or a clean handoff. beginWithCaller preserves protected input/search/checkpoint/certificate tickets and borrows the basis descriptor under those caller roots; archive/verification/wire must be vacant reserved roles at entry. takeClean transfers the sole Arena, achieved Usage, exact protected CallerRoots, admission credit ledger and machine overflow receipt once, leaving only Usage in the returned state. General refinement, native physical-memory qualification, external Product effect-ticket bridge and locked SDK acceptance remain separately required."},"kind":"definition","name":"boundary","parameters":[],"result":{"kind":"string"}}],"spec":"lexlean/semantic-module/1"}}
